- **precision-recall-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when ranked per-gene scores from competing methods must be compared against a known ground-truth gene set (simulated or curated) to quantify ranking accuracy via AUPRC and early-precision metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-benchmark-evaluation--27d72b236be8631a))
- **precision-recall-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a predicted sample-specific transcriptome (e.g., name_aptardi.gtf) must be quantitatively scored against gold-standard polyA sites to confirm cross-dataset generalizability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-benchmark-evaluation--1b73786d854e64a0))
- **precision-recall-benchmark-evaluation-of-loop-callers**: Use when comparing called chromatin loop sets from scHi-C loop-calling tools against a merged bulk-Hi-C/HiChIP reference list to rank methods by precision, recall, and F1 across subsampling levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-benchmark-evaluation-of-loop-callers))
- **precision-recall-benchmark-tabulation**: Use when comparing insertion-caller outputs (VCFs of NRS events &gt;300 bp) against a truth callset and you need per-tool precision/recall values, e.g. for a sensitivity-vs-coverage curve or a head-to-head benchmark table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-benchmark-tabulation))
- **precision-recall-benchmarking**: Use when you have sequencing reads from communities with known ground-truth taxonomic profiles (e.g. ten HBC mock communities) and must quantify which classifier's read-level assignments and abundance estimates are most precise and sensitive. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-benchmarking))
- **precision-recall-caveat-analysis**: Use when a GRN method's aggregate precision-recall curve looks acceptable and you need to check whether that summary hides poor edge-rank recovery for individual regulators in the gold standard. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-caveat-analysis))
- **precision-recall-computation**: Use when evaluating peak list alignment quality against known ground-truth alignments, e.g. comparing aligners (RANSAC vs Join) across LC-MS fractions at several RT tolerances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precision-recall-computation))
- **precision-recall-computation-against-ground-truth-transcripts**: Use when evaluating transcript reconstruction on simulated targeted Iso-Seq data where the ground-truth isoform set is known, e.g. comparing IsoCon against ICE precision/recall across read depths for multigene families like TSPY, HSFY, DAZ. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-computation-against-ground-truth-transcripts))
- **precision-recall-curve-analysis**: Use when comparing loop-calling predictions (e.g., GILoop vs. Peakachu) or evaluating a transferred model on K562/HeLa-S3 Hi-C data, and you need threshold-free precision-recall comparison against a defined ground-truth loop set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-curve-analysis))
- **precision-recall-curve-analysis**: Use when comparing similarity metrics (e.g. TransExION, Cosine, Modified Cosine, Spec2Vec, MS2DeepScore) on query-reference MS/MS spectrum pairs and the goal is analogue-retrieval performance, not a single correlation coefficient. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precision-recall-curve-analysis))
- **precision-recall-curve-analysis**: Use when comparing variant-calling or classification tools against a known ground truth, and you need cutoff-independent metrics (AUPRC/AUROC) plus PR/ROC curves across simulation settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-curve-analysis--77244276e13ed78f))
- **precision-recall-curve-analysis**: Use when inferred GRN edge lists (TF, Gene, importance from PIDC, GENIE3, GRNBoost2, etc.) must be evaluated against a ground-truth GRN to rank inference methods by precision@k and AUPRC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-curve-analysis--969c822e816aa475))
- **precision-recall-curve-auprc-evaluation**: Use when a reconstructed GRN (TF/Gene/importance rankings) must be scored against an imposed or curated ground truth GRN, and ranked edges need precision-recall quantification with a random-predictor baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-curve-auprc-evaluation))
- **precision-recall-curve-computation**: Use when two or more open-chromatin (or ChIP/CUT&Tag) peak files must be benchmarked against a gold-standard reference peak set, and a precision-recall plot is needed to contrast technologies such as ATAC-seq vs DNase-seq. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-curve-computation))
- **precision-recall-curve-evaluation**: Use when you have computationally called nucleosome dyad positions plus independent, high-precision ground-truth dyad annotations (e.g., Brogaard et al. chemical cleavage) and need to quantify positioning accuracy via precision-recall. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-curve-evaluation))
- **precision-recall-curve-evaluation**: Use when per-tool m6A confidence scores have been assigned to fixed genome bins (via --binLength) and you need to quantify detection-tool performance (precision, recall, AUPRC, 95% CIs) against a gold-standard peaks bed file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-curve-evaluation))
- **precision-recall-curve-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking de novo sequencing tools (Novor, pNovo 3, DeepNovo, SMSNet, PointNovo, Casanovo) against a peptide-spectrum-match ground truth and you need threshold-swept precision-recall AUCs to rank tools per enzyme. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precision-recall-curve-evaluation))
- **precision-recall-curve-evaluation**: Use when you have ranked enhancer–gene interaction scores (e.g., STARE_ABCpp gABC or regular ABC output) and binary ground-truth labels from CRISPRi perturbation datasets, and need to quantify or compare predictive accuracy via AUPRC/ROC AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-curve-evaluation--f1fd6a9bfc2f3455))
- **precision-recall-curve-evaluation**: Use when comparing gene co-expression detection methods (e.g., CS-CORE, Pearson, Spearman, locCSN, normalisr, NoiseRegularization) that output p-values, using a known ground-truth sparse co-expression network as the reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-curve-evaluation--f4a9163db5cca00c))
- **precision-recall-curve-evaluation**: Use when comparing discriminative vs. hierarchy-corrected cell-type classifiers (independent, IR, TPR, BNC) on labeled bulk or single-cell RNA-seq with Cell Ontology term labels, and average precision or PR curves are the scoring metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-curve-evaluation--6cda255be5ee0251))
- **precision-recall-curve-generation-and-interpretation**: Use when you have computed spectral similarity scores from multiple methods (e.g., MS2DeepScore, Spec2Vec, modified cosine) on the same set of spectrum pairs, have assigned ground-truth structural similarity labels (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-curve-generation-and-interpretation) · 2 copies)
- **precision-recall-curve-generation-for-ranking-tasks**: Use when when you have computed similarity scores (e.g., MS2DeepScore, Spec2Vec, modified Cosine) between pairs of spectra or compounds and want to compare their ability to retrieve chemically related pairs. Apply this skill if you have ground-truth structural similarity labels (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-curve-generation-for-ranking-tasks) · 3 copies)
- **precision-recall-curve-metric-extraction**: Use when benchmarking pathway enrichment methods against known true-positive pathways (e.g. similarity_sample-activated terms) and you need a recall-fixed precision summary under a specified noise level such as η=0.25. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-curve-metric-extraction))
- **precision-recall-driver-recovery-scoring**: Use when evaluating how well a network reconstruction method recovers known driver genes (e.g., IntOGen) from mutated-gene seed sets, requiring precision/recall comparison against baseline methods under k-fold cross-validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-driver-recovery-scoring))
- **precision-recall-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking an RT alignment or feature correspondence tool on simulated LC-MS data where the true correspondences are known from the perturbation, and per-shift-magnitude precision/recall curves are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precision-recall-evaluation))
- **precision-recall-evaluation-of-linking-information**: Use when comparing aligners or alignment-free linking methods on simulated reads where the true read-to-contig links are known, and you must report precision, recall, and runtime per tool. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-evaluation-of-linking-information))
- **precision-recall-evaluation-of-modification-callers**: Use when you have reads with per-read ground-truth modification labels (e.g. synthetic oligos where even-cycle GGACU positions are known modified) and need to compare modification callers by read-level precision-recall curves and PR AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-evaluation-of-modification-callers))
- **precision-recall-evaluation-of-variant-callers**: Use when a newly generated SV call set (e.g., from tumor/control long-read data) must be quantified as true/false positives against a published high-confidence somatic SV truth set to report precision and recall. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-evaluation-of-variant-callers))
- **precision-recall-f1-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a variant-calling benchmark needs standardized precision/recall/F1 numbers for ONT callsets (e.g. HG002 k12/k14 BAMs) against a GIAB truth set with stratification masking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-f1-benchmark-evaluation))
- **precision-recall-f1-benchmark-scoring**: Use when a pipeline's taxonomy assignments must be validated against a mock community with known member taxa, and comparable quality metrics (precision, recall, F1) to prior benchmarks are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/precision-recall-f1-benchmark-scoring))
- **precision-recall-f1-computation**: Use when comparing called binding sites from CLIP-seq methods against independently ascertained truth sets (e.g., chrM binding, splice sites, known motifs) to quantify which method recovers true RBP targets . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-f1-computation))
- **precision-recall-f1-computation**: Use when benchmarking SNV-calling VCFs (miniSNV, Clair3, Longshot) against GIAB truth sets for HG002/HG003/HG004 ONT data and you need per-sample precision, recall, and F1 within GIAB v3.3 stratification regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-f1-computation--03fcde3c0b5041af))
- **precision-recall-f1-computation**: Use when benchmarking a variant caller's VCF output against a GIAB HG001 truth set and you need whole-genome or per-chromosome precision, recall, and F1-score to compare callers or verify reported metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-f1-computation--cae414cbf8cfe00f))
- **precision-recall-f1-computation**: Use when benchmarking SV callers whose outputs are VCFs against a simulated or GIAB truth set, and you need per-SV-type (insertion vs deletion) recall, precision, and F1 to rank methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-f1-computation--34301c4e1ff13cb9))
- **precision-recall-f1-computation**: Use when you need to benchmark m6A detection tool calls against a gold-standard peaks BED file, counting TP/FP/FN per bin to derive precision, recall and F1 at a chosen threshold set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-f1-computation--7e948fd0f315d301))
- **precision-recall-f1-computation**: Use when a mock-community or simulated metagenomic benchmark provides known ground-truth abundances and you must score inferred gene-cluster abundance calls across a MASH-threshold × Bowtie2-mode grid. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precision-recall-f1-computation--2978ca7b27819254))
- **precision-recall-f1-computation**: Use when you have ranked candidate annotation lists per spectrum (e.g. ISDB-DNP, Sirius, MS-Finder outputs) and ground-truth structures, and need to compare tool performance or quantify the effect of re-ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precision-recall-f1-computation--731cebf8e026f939))
- **precision-recall-f1-computation**: Use when comparing per-cell predictions of malignant-cell identification tools (marker-based or CNV-based) against a published ground-truth label vector, and accuracy, precision, recall, and F1 with wall-clock runtime must be reported. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-f1-computation--72173c6a46da3ac9))
- **precision-recall-f1-computation**: Use when comparing trajectory inference methods (e.g. STREAM, Monocle2, ElPiGraph) on a branching single-cell dataset where sorted population labels serve as ground truth, to rank methods by per-marker precision/recall/F1 on lineage-specific branches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-f1-computation--ebceeebf6e577873))
- **precision-recall-f1-evaluation**: Use when an evaluator's reported error coordinates must be scored against simulated or known ground-truth error loci to produce per-error-class recall, precision, and F1 values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-f1-evaluation--cf8abe460bb7f2bb))
- **precision-recall-f1-evaluation**: Use when SV calls (e.g., minisv calls &gt;=10000 bp from pangenome or linear-reference alignments) must be quantitatively compared against a benchmark truth set to report precision, recall, and F1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-f1-evaluation--86c547370ab2ba18))
- **precision-recall-f1-evaluation**: Use when you have variant/insertion callsets (VCF) and a curated truth set (e.g., 5,630 HGSVC TE insertions) and need to quantify detection performance as precision/recall/F1, or compare tools head-to-head. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-f1-evaluation--2cc734bb45edfe1a))
- **precision-recall-f1-evaluation**: Use when comparing taxonomic profiling tools (e.g. MetaPhlAn 4 vs MetaPhlAn 3, mOTUs 2.6, Bracken 2.5) on synthetic metagenomes such as CAMI 2 or SynPhlAn where the ground-truth species composition is known. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/precision-recall-f1-evaluation))
- **precision-recall-f1-evaluation**: Use when comparing robustness of pathway-ranking methods on metabolomics intensity matrices where peaks may be missing or noisy — specifically when you have a full-data reference ranking and subsampled reruns (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precision-recall-f1-evaluation))
- **precision-recall-f1-evaluation**: Use when comparing differential network inference methods (e.g., BoostDiff vs GENIE3, ARACNE, RF Difftrees, EBcoexpress) on data with known ground-truth edges and you need precision/recall/F1 per network-size setting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-f1-evaluation))
- **precision-recall-f1-evaluation-of-sv-callers**: Use when evaluating or reproducing deletion SV caller performance on long-read data (simulated Sim_A/Sim_B at 90x/65x/45x/20x, or HG002 real data) where F1 score against a truth set must be computed and compared across tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-f1-evaluation-of-sv-callers--7cab61981456ea4e))
- **precision-recall-f1-evaluation-of-sv-callers**: Use when you need to quantitatively compare SV caller outputs against benchmark truth sets (e.g. GIAB samples like NA19240, HG00514, HG00733) and report DEL/INS precision, recall and F1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-f1-evaluation-of-sv-callers--7f683348b5b678e4))
- **precision-recall-f1-t**: Evaluates a model's ability to identify and rank key moments (shots) in soccer match videos for summarization. It measures how well the model selects representative content when constrained to match the exact duration of a human-curated highlight summary. Use when the user has predictions and gold and needs to compute F1 Score@$T$. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/precision-recall-f1-t) · ★3)
- **precision-recall-fmeasure-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when an LC-HRMS feature matching or alignment result must be validated against a ground-truth reference feature list, and TP/FN counts plus precision, recall and F-measure need to be reproduced or compared to a published benchmark (e.g., Table 3 values). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precision-recall-fmeasure-benchmark-evaluation))
- **precision-recall-fmeasure-benchmarking**: Use when you have simulated RNA-seq count data with known DE/non-DE gene labels and need to compare DEG-calling consensus strategies (vote thresholds of 1–4 or combined-FDR) by precision, recall and F-measure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-fmeasure-benchmarking))
- **precision-recall-fmeasure-calculation-per-taxonomic-rank**: Use when comparing taxonomic classifiers (e.g. Centrifuge, Kraken2, mothur, RDP, SPINGO, BLCA, QIIME 2 Sklearn) on sequences with a known ground-truth community composition and you need rank-wise precision/recall/F-measure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-fmeasure-calculation-per-taxonomic-rank))
- **precision-recall-fscore-benchmarking**: Use when you need to judge whether an scRNA-seq preprocessing step (e.g., dropout imputation with scImpute, MAGIC, or SAVER) improves downstream differential-expression agreement with a bulk RNA-seq ground truth, via F-score comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/precision-recall-fscore-benchmarking))
- **precision-recall-fscore-calculation**: Use when evaluating chromatographic peak quality filtering: you have a detected peak list with quality metric values and a ground truth peak list, and need Recall/Precision/F-Score at varied metric cutoff thresholds. - **precision-recall-jaccard-metric-computation**: Use when evaluating or reproducing performance ranges of CYP450 metabolite prediction tools against a curated ground-truth metabolite dataset (e.g. EBoMD2 hold-out compounds in 60Reactants.sdf). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precision-recall-jaccard-metric-computation))
- **precision-recall-metric-computation**: Use when evaluating an eDNA metabarcoding pipeline on a mock community with a known taxonomic ground truth and you need quantitative precision/recall/F1 per clustering or ASV-inference condition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/precision-recall-metric-computation--3512ad2ecc1c3859))
- **precision-recall-metric-computation**: Use when evaluating a metagenomic read classifier (e.g., MetaMaps, Kraken, Centrifuge) on datasets with known ground truth, to quantify strain- and species-level assignment accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/precision-recall-metric-computation--e79e8db91f40cf80))
- **precision-recall-metric-computation**: Use when evaluating spectral-search identification performance: you have query and reference peak lists (e.g. QqQ vs QqTOF ESI-MS2 spectra), per-pair similarity scores, and need precision/recall/F at a fixed score cutoff such as 0.6. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precision-recall-metric-computation))
- **precision-recall-metric-computation**: Use when comparing phosphorylation-site classifiers on imbalanced positive/negative site labels and you need recall and AUPRC (rather than accuracy) from per-site predicted probability outputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/precision-recall-metric-computation))

([line7works/tony-skills](https://github.com/line7works/tony-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/precon-v2/skills/precon-v2))
- **precondition-check**: Use BEFORE emitting any recommendation, suggestion, "we should X", design idea, or fix proposal — and before accepting any factual premise embedded in a request (e.g. "since the library doesn't support X..."). Fires in casual conversation and asides, not just build mode. Verify the precondition a suggestion rests on before saying it. ([kyletabor/claude_plugins](https://github.com/kyletabor/claude_plugins/tree/HEAD/fable-mode/skills/precondition-check) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **precondition-inference**: Identify required preconditions from guards, validation, and context ([isdlc/isdlc-knowledge-service](https://github.com/isdlc/isdlc-knowledge-service/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reverse-engineer/precondition-inference))
- **preconnect**: Use when auditing slow page loads, heavy assets, or rendering delays related to Use preconnect for critical third-party origins. Verify the actual bottleneck in DevTools, Lighthouse, or field data before recommending changes. ([thedaviddias/Front-End-Checklist](https://github.com/thedaviddias/Front-End-Checklist/tree/HEAD/skills/preconnect) · ★75k · 3 copies)
- **preconstruction-en**: Runs a pre-sales (preconstruction) research study of a construction or construction-IT idea using the 5-stage AI-assisted pre-sales method: a scoping interview, research streams (business analysis, marketing, technical feasibility, whether AI is needed inside the product), a skeptical audit, and one consolidated report with a pitch. Use when the user wants to research, validate or package a project idea, size the market and competitors, check feasibility, or decide whether the product needs AI. English version; Russian mirror is preconstruction-ru. ([Barbashin1970/PRECONSTRUCTION](https://github.com/Barbashin1970/PRECONSTRUCTION/tree/HEAD/skills/preconstruction-en) · ★1)
- **preconstruction-estimating**: Guides PMs through preconstruction activities including bid leveling, value engineering, GMP development, and buyout procedures. ([Construction-Gamma/construction-gamma-skills](https://github.com/Construction-Gamma/construction-gamma-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/preconstruction-estimating) · ★16)
- **preconstruction-ru**: Проводит пресейл-исследование (preconstruction, presale) строительной или ИТ-для-стройки идеи по методике «5 этапов пресейла с ИИ»: интервью для скоупинга, потоки-исследователи (бизнес-анализ, маркетинг, техэкспертиза, нужен ли ИИ внутри продукта), скептический аудит и единый отчёт с питчем. Использовать, когда надо проработать или упаковать идею проекта, оценить рынок, конкурентов и реализуемость или решить, нужен ли ИИ в продукте. Русская версия, зеркало — preconstruction-en. ([Barbashin1970/PRECONSTRUCTION](https://github.com/Barbashin1970/PRECONSTRUCTION/tree/HEAD/skills/preconstruction-ru) · ★1)
- **preconv**: Use this skill when the user wants to use preconv, cli tool: preconv. ([javimosch/supercli](https://github.com/javimosch/supercli/tree/HEAD/plugins/preconv/skills/quickstart) · ★58)
- **precoro**: Precoro integration. Manage data, records, and automate workflows. Use when the user wants to interact with Precoro data. ([membranedev/application-skills](https://github.com/membranedev/application-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/precoro) · ★273 · 2 copies)
- **precoro-automation**: Automate Precoro tasks via Rube MCP (Composio). Always search tools first for current schemas. ([ComeOnOliver/skillshub](https://github.com/ComeOnOliver/skillshub/tree/HEAD/skills/ComposioHQ/awesome-claude-skills/precoro-automation) · ★67 · 59 copies)
- **precoro-automation**: Automate Precoro tasks via Rube MCP (Composio). Always search tools first for current schemas. Use when you need help with precoro automation. ([anubhavg-icpl/vibe](https://github.com/anubhavg-icpl/vibe/tree/HEAD/skills/composio-precoro-automation) · ★13)
- **precoro-automation**: Automate Precoro tasks via Composio MCP (Composio). Always search tools first for current schemas. ([EosBot/awesome-zoocode-skills](https://github.com/EosBot/awesome-zoocode-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/composio/precoro-automation))
- **precos-transferencia-lei-14596**: Usar para pesquisar ou analisar: Preços de Transferência (Lei 14.596/2023 + OECD Guidelines). Combinar com o protocolo LegalBR e conferir fontes oficiais antes de aplicar. ([prof-ramos/skills](https://github.com/prof-ramos/skills/tree/HEAD/legalbr/materia/tributario/precos-transferencia-lei-14596))
- **precursor-adduct-type-filtering**: Use when building benchmark MS/MS datasets from raw HMDB, GNPS, or CASMI spectra and you must restrict entries to protonated or deprotonated precursor types before counting qualified spectra and compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-adduct-type-filtering))
- **precursor-and-noise-filtering**: Use when you have loaded raw tandem MS spectra (in MGF, mzML, or similar format) and need to prepare them for peptide identification, spectral library matching, or intensity-based analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-and-noise-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-centroid-consistency-validation**: Use when processing large batches of raw LC–MS/MS spectra (e.g. mined from MassIVE GNPS .mzML experiments) where fragment centroids must be validated against precursor m/z prior to ML training or spectrum library curation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-centroid-consistency-validation))
- **precursor-charge-aware-spectra-pair-sampling**: Use when training the GLEAMS spectral embedding network on MassIVE-KB PSMs and you need balanced same-peptide/mismatched spectrum pairs stratified by precursor charge for contrastive-loss training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-charge-aware-spectra-pair-sampling))
- **precursor-charge-state-filtering**: Use when one peptide ID appears on multiple MS2 spectra with different precursor charge states (e.g. z=2 vs z=3 in an iTRAQ 4-plex experiment) and the goal is a charge-resolved spectral overlay comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/precursor-charge-state-filtering))
- **precursor-fragment-ion-pairing**: Use when you have raw LC-MS/MS data files (mzML/mzXML format from Thermo, Waters, or Bruker instruments) and a list of target compounds defined by precursor m/z values (and optionally retention time windows). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-fragment-ion-pairing) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-fragment-pairing-for-mrm**: Use when when designing a targeted lipidomics experiment and you have lipid species definitions (including chain composition and adducts) but need to configure precursor–fragment transitions for MRM or PRM acquisition on a Thermo QExactive HF, Agilent QTOF, or compatible high-resolution or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-fragment-pairing-for-mrm) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-interference-qc-score-calculation**: Use when quantifying iTRAQ, TMT (up to 11-plex), or TMTpro (up to 16-plex) labeled peptides from mzML files plus PSM hit files and you need QC scores (ms1PrecSignal, peptideIntensityScore, msnTotalSignal) to filter compression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/precursor-interference-qc-score-calculation))
- **precursor-ion-chromatogram-extraction**: Use when you have raw DIA mass spectrometry data files (.raw, .d, or .wiff formats) from timsTOF, TripleTOF, or Orbitrap instruments and need to extract precursor ion chromatograms for quality assessment or downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-ion-chromatogram-extraction) · 3 copies)
- **precursor-ion-information-parsing**: Use when an mzXML (or other supported peak-list/XML-based) MS file must be parsed in Java to recover scan records with precursor ion details and peak arrays for downstream proteomics analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/precursor-ion-information-parsing))
- **precursor-ion-isolation-windowing**: Use when processing SWATH-MS (Sequential Windowed Acquisition of all Theoretical Mass-spectra) raw data files (mzML or vendor format) for untargeted metabolomics, specifically before attempting to deconvolute overlapping MS/MS spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-ion-isolation-windowing) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-ion-mass-error-calculation**: Use when performing omics-agnostic MS quality control on raw MS runs (LC-MS, LC-IMS-MS, DDA/DIA, or direct infusion) and you need per-ion error metrics (mass, RT, AT/1/K0) plus spectral counts and TIC to detect instrument drift or outliers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-ion-mass-error-calculation))
- **precursor-ion-metadata-extraction**: Use when mzML files must be parsed in Java to recover per-spectrum precursor m/z and charge from spectrum metadata, alongside peak m/z and intensity arrays. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/precursor-ion-metadata-extraction))
- **precursor-ion-mode-data-processing**: Use when AEMS plate data was acquired in full-scan precursor ion (PI) mode and all wells of a 384-well plate must be processed (peak detection, shot-to-peak association, area quantification) or benchmarked against the reported &lt;30 s runtime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-ion-mode-data-processing))
- **precursor-ion-overlap-analysis**: Use when you have a RegFilter-exported scheduled precursor list (.csv) and the matching MZmine MS1 feature table for the same LC-MS runs, and need to report the percentage of SPL ions present in MS1 and count SPL/DDA fragmented-precursor overlaps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-ion-overlap-analysis))
- **precursor-ion-peak-exclusion**: Use when preparing raw MS/MS peak lists for MMSA spectral alignment and uninformative peaks near the precursor ion (within ±17 m/z) must be excluded before top-6 intensity filtering and square-root transformation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-ion-peak-exclusion))
- **precursor-ion-peak-metrics-analysis**: Use when QC'ing a batch of LC-MS/LC-IMS-MS or infusion runs (Agilent '.d', Thermo '.raw', Bruker '.d', mzML) by profiling precursor-ion errors and flagging anomalous runs per metric and sample group rather than eyeballing spectra manually. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-ion-peak-metrics-analysis))
- **precursor-ion-tolerance-sweep-benchmarking**: Use when you need to characterize how runtime of a mass spectral search tool (e.g. VInSMoC) scales as precursor ion tolerance increases, or to compare exact vs. variable search cost head-to-head. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-ion-tolerance-sweep-benchmarking))
- **precursor-list-formatting-for-dda**: Use when you have generated a lipid spectral library (lipid identities, adducts, m/z values, fragmentation patterns) and your downstream analysis requires DDA acquisition on an Orbitrap instrument using Excalibur software. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-list-formatting-for-dda) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-m-z-based-filtering**: Use when you have an unknown MS/MS query spectrum with a known or measured precursor m/z value and need to search a spectral library (local or public: GNPS, MASSBANK, DrugBANK) to annotate the compound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-m-z-based-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-mass-annotation**: Use when you hold a list of precursor accurate masses (e.g., oxPC precursor m/z values in a CSV) and need compound-level annotations — species name, molecular formula, expert-system score — from CMM 3.0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-annotation))
- **precursor-mass-calculation**: Use when when you have a compound's SMILES string or molecular formula and need to determine the expected precursor ion m/z for comparison against observed spectra, particularly before applying formula-based denoising, entropy similarity scoring, or denoising search against reference libraries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-mass-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-mass-candidate-filtering**: Use when searching query tandem mass spectra against large structure databases (e.g., PubChem, DNP) and you must first restrict candidate molecules by observed precursor mass before running fragment-matching scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-candidate-filtering))
- **precursor-mass-candidate-screening**: Use when a set of LC-MS precursor queries (measured m/z, optional RT) needs candidate structures from a database narrowed down, and orthogonal predicted properties (RT, CCS) are available to re-rank candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-candidate-screening))
- **precursor-mass-consistency-checking**: Use when you need to quantify how often adduct annotations in a mass spectral library (e.g., GNPS MGF spectra with SMILES) are wrong or can be corrected from SMILES plus precursor m/z alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-consistency-checking))
- **precursor-mass-defect-computation**: Use when MS/MS spectra of suspected chemically modified metabolites (methylation, acetylation, phosphorylation, hydroxylation) must be associated with library spectra of their non-modified parent compounds by measuring precursor and fragment mass shifts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-defect-computation))
- **precursor-mass-difference-alignment**: Use when comparing two tandem MS spectra (e.g. a library spectrum vs. a query spectrum of a related molecule) and you need to score match quality including peaks shifted by the precursor mass difference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-difference-alignment))
- **precursor-mass-difference-analysis**: Use when a large corpus of unidentified spectra has been clustered (e.g., GLEAMS clusters with medoid indexes) and you need to annotate representative spectra via open modification search and derive the mass-difference profile. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-difference-analysis))
- **precursor-mass-difference-calculation**: Use when when comparing two MS/MS spectra using modified cosine similarity and the precursor m/z values differ, indicating potential neutral losses, adduct variations, or analogs with different substituents. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-mass-difference-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-mass-eligibility-filtering**: Use when assembling analog compound-pair benchmarks from MS/MS libraries — you have enumerated pairs and must enforce precursor mass and adduct eligibility before substructure matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-eligibility-filtering))
- **precursor-mass-feature-tracking**: Use when an unannotated molecular feature defined by precursor m/z (e.g., m/z 266.138, 280.153, 294.169) or an MS/MS spectrum must be searched across tissues or mapped from an animal model into public human metabolomics studies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-feature-tracking))
- **precursor-mass-filtering**: Use when after retrieving top-scoring library candidates from a full MS2Deepscore comparison, but before or during final re-ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-mass-filtering) · 3 copies)
- **precursor-mass-filtering-spectrum-search**: Use when matching EI-MS query spectra against a million-scale in-silico library and the query's molecular mass is known or estimable, so candidates can be restricted to a ±5 Da mass window to improve recall@1/recall@10. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-filtering-spectrum-search))
- **precursor-mass-neutral-loss-calculation**: Use when when analyzing tandem mass spectra (MS/MS data) and you want to incorporate neutral loss patterns—characteristic mass losses from molecular precursors—into your spectral similarity or feature representation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-mass-neutral-loss-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-mass-ppm-filtering**: Use when dereplicating untargeted LC-MS2 features (.mzML input) against public spectral libraries (GNPS, HMDB, MassBank) inside MAW-R and you need reproducible precursor-ppm and similarity-score filtering of candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-ppm-filtering))
- **precursor-mass-preselection-filtering**: Use when running modified cosine (or similar score-based) analogue searching against a large library (e.g., 302,514 GNPS spectra) and you need to bound pairwise score computations by precursor m/z proximity, or when benchmarking the runtime cost of such preselection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-preselection-filtering))
- **precursor-mass-shift-analog-detection**: Use when query MS/MS spectra from molecular networking or dereplication jobs show putative analogs of known compounds: the goal is to find library hits whose fragment peaks are systematically shifted by the precursor mass difference δ ± the user-defined fragment mass tolerance, rather than. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-shift-analog-detection))
- **precursor-mass-stratified-performance-analysis**: Use when benchmarking formula identification tools on tandem MS spectra and you need to quantify how accuracy degrades with increasing precursor m/z, especially for compounds above 800 Da. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-stratified-performance-analysis))
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-alignment**: Use when untargeted LC-HRMS feature tables from a sample (e.g., AFFF) must be aligned to solvent-blank feature tables by precursor m/z to distinguish real sample features from background contamination. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-alignment))
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-benchmarking**: Use when benchmarking dereplication tools (DEREPLICATOR+, DEREPLICATOR-G, DEREPLICATOR) on spectra with known metabolite annotations and you need to measure how precursor mass tolerance settings (e.g. 0.5 Da vs 0.02 Da) change top-rank identification rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-benchmarking))
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering**: Use when ranking candidate molecules for MS/MS spectra: precursor m/z must be used to restrict the 520,083-compound reference database before embedding similarity comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering--f9f662e389a4d0ac))
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering**: Use when annotating LC–MS/MS metabolite peaks in plant metabolomics data and you need candidate metabolites filtered by MS1 precursor m/z tolerance (e.g., 25 ppm) prior to MS/MS and RT scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering--700d3727fb385ea5))
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering**: Use when computing a sparse pairwise spectrum similarity matrix or nearest-neighbor candidate list and only pairs within a precursor mass tolerance (ppm or Dalton) are valid, as in MS/MS clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering--df20ed52326ac6ef))
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering**: Use when after cosine similarity or dot-product scoring of experimental MS/MS spectra against a reference library, when you need to eliminate matches whose precursor m/z values deviate too far from the experimental precursor m/z, thereby improving specificity of library matching before final. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering**: Use when implementing de novo peptide prediction from MS/MS spectra and you need to constrain beam-search candidates to peptides consistent with the observed precursor m/z, accounting for isotope-error offsets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-filtering--ebf7bba78725562d))
- **precursor-mass-tolerance-matching**: Use when you have experimental MGF-format MS/MS spectra (e.g., human blood metabolomics) plus one or more reference MS/MS libraries, and you need to count how many spectra can be annotated by library search under cosine similarity thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-tolerance-matching))
- **precursor-mass-window-filtering**: Use when annotating unassigned query MS/MS spectra against a reference library: before Denoising Search scoring, filter library entries whose precursor m/z or molecular formula mass lies outside the instrument's precursor mass accuracy window. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mass-window-filtering))
- **precursor-metadata-extraction-from-peak-lists**: Use when a peak list file in PKL (or a related text format like DTA, MS2, MGF) needs header parsing to recover precursor m/z, charge, and intensity fields, and per-spectrum records with peak lists must be assembled. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/precursor-metadata-extraction-from-peak-lists))
- **precursor-mz-candidate-filtering**: Use when matching unknown query MS/MS spectra against a large spectral library (e.g., ~76,000–95,000 AllPositive/GNPS spectra) and full pairwise scoring would be too slow: filter candidates by precursor m/z before computing similarity scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/precursor-mz-candidate-filtering))
- **precursor-mz-candidate-retrieval**: Use when you have an MS/MS analyte precursor mass and need a putative compound candidate list from a metabolite database for annotation or fingerprint-score ranking.
